## Terra Provisor

### Wiki
Corpus
Hybrid air-ground unit, armed with a Tetra rifle and a devastating visor mounted beam weapon.
Terra Provisors are Corpus enemy units encountered in the Orb Vallis on Venus, equipped with a Tetra rifle and utilizing jetpacks to engage in aerial combat.
Tactic
These units hover in the air with its jetpack shooting at any players in sight with its Tetra rifle. Provisors can also shoot a blue laser beam out of their helmets pushing the player back dealing massive damage.
Farming Locations
These are based on opinions and may not be 100% true. These should be viewed as advice for finding the enemies until better facts are proven. For endless missions, players may need to stay in-mission for a while until these enemies start spawning.
Orb Vallis
Freeing camps from Corpus for Solaris United will spawn multiple during the mini mobile defense mission, a good way to farm the Memoriam Purple Pigment.
**Tips:**
Players can shoot their jetpacks to ground the Provisor.

## The Sergeant

### Wiki
The Sergeant, formerly known as Sgt. Nef Anyo before Update 16.4, is the final boss of Phobos system. He is in charge of the Corpus Solar Rail systems, using them to obtain Cryopods with dormant Warframes for resale to rogue scientists and splinter groups. When confronted by the player during the mission, he has a very aggressive tone towards the Tenno and warns the player that they are making a fatal mistake by attempting his assassination. He also may demand information regarding Orokin secrets, or make fun of the player's Warframe or teammates.
He can be found on the mission Iliad, Phobos. A successful assassination awards Mag component blueprints. The Sergeant has an increased chance to drop Morphics upon death.
Behavior
Lanka: A charged sniper rifle that deals high damage.
Flash Bang: Throws a flash bang onto the ground, rendering himself invisible. Despite this, the objective marker will give away his position when he becomes visible again.
Strategy
The Sergeant is essentially a stronger Sniper Crewman with a tendency to exploit hide-and-snipe tactics as opposed to the head-on-assault of most bosses.
Aside from depleting his shields and health, there is little in the way of strategy to the overall battle. As the Sergeant is always vulnerable to damage, the Tenno only need to attack him until he is defeated.
Health: 500 | Shield: 1000 | Armor: 150 | Faction: Corpus

## Tia Mayn

### Wiki
Corpus
Tia supports her allies by providing them with effective exit strategies when a deal takes a turn for the worse. These strategies often leave the other party with significant medical fees.
Behavior
Tia uses her Ohma to attack any players in sight. Each attack can stagger players on hit.
She will block attacks from players, reducing damage dealt to her.
She also shares with other Claims Investigation Brokers the ability to deploy Translocators, which provides teleportation for the Anyo Corp Brokers. Each Translocator will remain deployed until a player destroys it.
Tia's signature ability, Cloak, makes her invisible. It will be dispelled if she is damaged.
She will sometimes deploy Slow orbs, much like Pelna Cade.

## Tomb Guardian
Guards the Tomb of Inaros.

### Wiki
Wild
Guards the Tomb of Inaros.
The Tomb Guardian is a miniboss fought during the Sands of Inaros quest. It appears when the second Sacred Vessel is returned.
**Notes:**
When defeated, the Guardian will split into two smaller versions, each with 1530 base health, i.e. 90% of the original Tomb Guardian's health.
Defeating a first level split Tomb Guardian will cause it to split again, each with 1445 base health, i.e. 85% of the original Tomb Guardian's health.
Defeating a second level split Tomb Guardian will cause it to split again, each with 1360 base health, i.e. 80% of the original Tomb Guardian's health.
Defeating a third level split Tomb Guardian will cause it to split again, each with 1275 base health, i.e. 75% of the original Tomb Guardian's health.
Tomb Guardians have 50% Damage Reduction against Puncture and Slash damage.
**Tips:**
Players can quickly become overwhelmed by large numbers of Tomb Guardians, each throwing rocks that do heavy damage, and potentially stun-locking a player to suffer multiple rock strikes and die.
Try to split and kite the group, the chamber where the vessel is found allows a player to take good cover and bullet jump to pick off targets while avoiding thrown boulders.
**Trivia:**
It resembles a larger version of one of Atlas's Rumblers, although sandstone colored and covered in sand.
Health: 2000 | Shield: 0 | Armor: 150 | Faction: Warframe

## Tomb Protector
Watches over the Tomb of Inaros.

### Wiki
Wild
Watches over the Tomb of Inaros.
The Tomb Protector is a copy of the Inaros Warframe that tests intruders to see if they are worthy of claiming Inaros' blueprints. It appears as a miniboss only on the final mission of the Sands of Inaros quest when the final Sacred Vessel is retrieved.
Tactics
After reaching 75% / 50% / 25% of its maximum health, the Tomb Protector will retreat into its sarcophagus and summon a Tomb Protector Effigy to fight while the Tomb Protector heals. Defeating the Tomb Protector Effigy will cause the Tomb Protector to reemerge from its sarcophagus. 2 Tomb Protector Effigies are summoned at when Tomb Protector reaches 25% health.
He can cast abilities, but with some slight differences:
Desiccation merely inflicts the damage over time and does not immobilize nor blind any targets caught in its wake.
Devour simply creates a Sand Shadow clone of the player.
As he is equipped with a Mutalist Cernos, he can create clouds that can proc Toxin damage.
Health: 8000 | Shield: 0 | Armor: 250 | Faction: Neutral

## Tomb Protector Effigy
Awoken by the Protector to help defend the Tomb of Inaros.

### Wiki
Wild
Awoken by the Protector to help defend the Tomb of Inaros.
The Tomb Protector Effigy is a giant stone golem made in the image of Inaros, encountered during the Sands of Inaros quest. Four of these can be found forming the centerpiece of the tomb altar guarding Inaros' remains, and will reanimate to fight the Tenno alongside the Tomb Protector in the quest's final mission.
Enemy Tactics
A Tomb Protector Effigy will reanimate to fight if players manage to force the Tomb Protector back into its sarcophagus. Only when the currently active Effigy is destroyed will the Tomb Protector come back out of hiding.
Two Tomb Protector Effigies will become active upon the third and final time that the Tomb Protector is forced into its sarcophagus.
The Tomb Protector Effigy is equipped with a Lesion as its primary weapon, preferring to attack players in melee.
If engaging players at range, the Effigy will attack with beams fired from its eyes, which deal Heat damage and proc.
The attacks of the Effigies can travel through the Rift Plane.
**Tips:**
It is possible to lead the Effigies out the temple to use the terrain to your advantage, but after a while they will squeeze through narrow crevices to reach you.
Health: 5500 | Shield: 0 | Armor: 250 | Faction: Neutral

## Torment
Arrogant and calculating

### Wiki
Arrogant and calculating
Torment is one of Shadow Stalker's Acolytes, first introduced during Operation: Shadow Debt. She takes the form of Mesa with Stalker's helmet, a Naberus shoulder piece, and some of Equinox's front tassels. She also uses an Eos chest plate for a codpiece, and the Eos spurs.
As of Update 29.5, Torment can now spawn in The Steel Path missions.
Tactics
Instead of Mesa's abilities, Torment uses Tempest Barrage and Tidal Surge and wields the Akvasto.
Quotes
"Alad V only wants profit, he is the enemy."
"This one has caused much suffering and yet you protect him... Why?"
"You have lost your way, Tenno, I will help you find it."
"I am not defeated."
**Notes:**
Torment was the first Acolyte to be brought back after being defeated. Uniquely, it was before Operation: Shadow Debt had even ended because of the Update 18.4.3 that tweaked the drop rate of mods wasn't released until after her first defeat.
Torment's Tempest Barrage deals corrosive procs to the player and staggers them on each hit
Torment's Tidal Surge dash clears status effects from herself

## Toxic Ancient
Heavy melee unit surrounded by poison cloud

### Wiki
Infested
Heavy melee unit surrounded by poison cloud.
Toxic Ancient is an Infested heavy unit that can use a special Toxin damage attack.
Toxic Ancients can be identified by the bright green glow from their heads, as well as their black and green bodies, and the spiny protrusions on their back.
Behavior
Toxic Ancient's normal form of attack is to melee the player with its right arm, dealing 45 Impact damage, scaled by enemy level.
If the player is not in melee range, the Toxic Ancient will do one of the following:
Charge at the player, running up to them and meleeing with its left arm, dealing 150 Impact damage, scaled by enemy level, and inflicting Knockdown.
Toxic Ancients can melee with their left arm without charging.
Use its grappling hook ability to reel players into melee range.
The grappling hook has a 1 second delay before being reeled back and inflicting Knockdown. During this time the player can cut the rope with a melee attack, or bullet jump or roll which will cause the Toxic Ancient to be dragged along.
Grappling hook is identical to that used by Scorpions.
Occasionally uses its Toxic Flail ability. Every hit of this attack is unblockable.
The first and second hit deals 150 Impact damage, scaled by enemy level and applies a Stagger.
The third hit does a slam, which deals 450 Toxin damage scaled by enemy level in an 7 meter radius, along with a Knockdown and a Toxin status effect.
Slam damage diminishes with distance.
Deals an additional 50 Impact damage, not scaled by enemy level, to players hit directly by the flail.
Blocking can prevent the Toxin status effect from being applied.
**Notes:**
Has 80% Toxin Damage Type resistance.
The crown on its back is a special body modifier that reduces damage by 50%.
Health: 400 | Shield: 0 | Armor: 0 | Faction: Infestation

## Trooper
Heavily armored, close range Lancer

### Wiki
Grineer
Troopers are the shotgun-wielding counterpart to the Lancer. Armed with various shotguns, they lack the accuracy and range of Lancers but are outfitted in more heavily armored yellow attire. This makes them comparatively more effective in close quarters.
Tactics
Troopers generally share the tactics of Lancers: hide, shoot and throw grenades. However, they often refrain to take cover and move closer to their targets with their shotgun, making them significantly more dangerous in close range.
Troopers will occasionally take cover and blind-fire at their targets, albeit with suffered accuracy; (a) if ever their cover is half of their standing height, almost completely covering most of their weak-spots, (b) if standing near a post or an end of a wall, they will rotate their weapons sideways, hiding most of their bodies until moving sideways reveal them.
They occasionally throw grenades, and when behind cover there is an increased likelihood they will throw one.
Farming Locations
These are based on opinions and may not be 100% true. These should be viewed as advice for finding the enemies until better facts are proven.
Trooper
Pantheon
Frontier Trooper
Pacific
**Notes:**
The Trooper Survivor is a friendly unit that is spawned along with Garv during Cambion Drift Bounties.

## Tusk Bolkor
The pride of the Tusk fleet, the Bolkor is the ideal platform from which to mount ground operations. Equipped with a high capacity rotary cannon, densely plated and with enough space to carry a platoon, the Bolkor holds its own raining down fire and dropping squad after squad at regular intervals.

### Wiki
Grineer
The pride of the Tusk fleet, the Bolkor is the ideal platform from which to mount ground operations. Equipped with a high capacity rotary canon, densely plated and with enough space to carry a platoon, the Bolkor holds its own raining down fire and dropping squad after squad at regular intervals.
The Bolkor is a Grineer gunship, that is used to bring in high numbers of reinforcements on Plains of Eidolon.
Tactics
Unlike the harmless Firbolgs, Bolkors are very effective at suppressing Tenno with their cannons, located under the ship. This minigun has its own hit-points and can be attacked with the player's weapons and powers. Once the minigun is disabled, the Bolkor usually retreats.
Passengers are protected by the Bolkor's hull and cannot be killed while in transit. However, they deploy much slower than from Firbolg and still are vulnerable while the rear ramp is lowered.
If the ship is left unattended, it will drop several waves of Grineer.
Engines and maneuver legs are somewhat vulnerable, however destroying the whole Bolkor would still take time.
Bolkors are more common in high level instances of the Plains, and can be found over the hills of Plains more frequently than near the shore.
Health: 10000 | Shield: 0 | Armor: 600 | Faction: Grineer

## Tusk Firbolg
In and out under extreme duress, the Firbolg handles lightning-fast infiltrations/extractions and lazy picket duty with equal aplomb.

### Wiki
Grineer
In and out under extreme duress, the Firbolg handles lightning fast infiltrations/extractions and lazy picket duty with equal aplomb.
The Firbolg is an unarmed Grineer shuttle that drops platoons of soldiers on the Plains of Eidolon.
Tactics
While Firbolgs do not pose a threat by themselves, their noisy punch-in warns about the incoming Grineer troopers that are perched atop their hull.
Passengers can be shot down individually, since they sit outside the hull desant-style, leaving them exposed to attack. This will count as a stealth kill.
Destroying a Firbolg before it reaches its dropoff point will kill any passengers who have not started their dismounting animation. Passengers killed this way will count toward any relevant bounty objectives (such as the Control Level in Liberation) but will not yield any affinity or drops.
Firbolgs are immune to many status effects, such as Slash, Impact, and Viral, but they are vulnerable to Heat and some others.
**Trivia:**
Non-interactive Firbolgs are present on many tilesets, indicating their widespread use for a variety of purposes. For example, they evacuate Capture and Defection targets on Grineer tilesets, and are seen being used to resupply the Kuva Fortress during The War Within quest.
Keeping with the theme of Grineer space vehicles having names from Irish mythology, these dropships take their name from the mythical Fir Bolg people of Ireland, their name translating to "Men of Bags" on account of previously being slaves.
The Firbolg appeared in the Awakening opening cinematic, sporting a more clean polish with Orokin decorations during its use in The Collapse.

## Tusk Seeker Drone
Identifies potential threats and calls in reinforcements.

### Wiki
colors of flares need confirmations
Grineer
For the drones launched by Councilor Vay Hek, see Orbital Strike Drone and Propaganda Drone.
Tusk Seeker Drones are Grineer flying drones with flapping, accordion-like wings, that are deployed by Tusk Grineer, namely: Tusk Lancers, Tusk Seekers, Tusk Predators, Tusk Thumpers, and Dargyn Pilots. They have the ability to call in reinforcements by firing off Fosfor flares, and will do so more quickly if the number of nearby Grineer dwindles. It is very easy to become overwhelmed by incoming forces if these drones and their masters are not dispatched in a timely manner. After they are deployed, their presence is indicated by the warbling electronic noises they make.
Reinforcement Variants
The Fosfor flare's smoke color indicates the type of reinforcements being summoned (ordered from least to most rare):
Red ◼: Additional Tusk Firbolg-platoon.
Purple ◼: Drop-pod platoon on flare location.
Violet ◼: Additional Tusk Bolkor-platoon.
Blue ◼: Tusk Dargyn/Tusk Shield Dargyn air support.
Green ◼: Tusk Ogma bomber support.
Reinforcement tier seems to increase based on kills per minute.
When a ship cannot reach the place where the flare landed, reinforcements do not come.
**Notes:**
The flares make beeping electronic noises, are generally shot ~20 m away from the drone, stick to any surface, and cannot be destroyed.
The drones will fire a flare for every ~4 killed Grineer, therefore a drone can spawn and not fire a flare.
The drones' health decays over time.
Only 1 drone may be active at once.
Seeker Drone seems to be the lowest-priority gear - often deployed after grenades, brunts, and other stuff.
The drones do not appear at nighttime.
Cannot be ragdolled or knocked prone by any means.
Cannot be pulled by Nidus's Larva, but can be pulled by Tether Grenades.

## Tusk Thumper
Grineer mobile defense platform. Pneumatic groundpounders, high-drop entrances and wide-bore cannons deliver the same simple message: boom.

### Wiki
Grineer
Grineer mobile defense platform. Pneumatic groundpounders, high-drop entrances and wide-bore cannons deliver the same simple message: boom.
The Tusk Thumper is a heavily-armored Grineer quadrupedal vehicle designed for heavy combat. They can be found in the Plains of Eidolon during daytime.
Stronger variants exist: the Tusk Thumper Bull, the Tusk Thumper Doma, and the Narmer Thumper Doma.
Tactics
The heavy armor on the Tusk Thumper makes its body impervious to all forms of attack. To deal damage to it, players must destroy the destructible armor plates on the Thumper's knees, which will reveal a green weak spot on each leg that can be attacked. Each weak spot constitutes a quarter of the Thumper's health, with weak spot closing up if its health pool is depleted. Thus, all 4 leg weak spots must be destroyed in order to kill the Thumper.
The Thumper possesses unusual agility for a unit of its size, capable of jumping high into the air to either reposition itself or attempt to slam down onto enemies beneath it. It is also capable of charging forward at great speeds, knocking over any enemies in its path.
Occasionally, the Thumper will brace its legs and then use its pneumatic groundpounder on its belly, which it slams into the ground repeatedly to create up to 5 radial shockwaves that deal damage and knockback in a large area. However, the Thumper is immobile in this state, making it easier for players to aim for its weak spots, particularly its "back" two legs that it typically keeps turning away from you as it moves around and turns to aim weapons at you. These shockwaves, as the physics of their source imply, are purely through the ground. Hovering above the ground, such as in an Archwing, or jumping to avoid them, will negate their damage and knockback, although the sounds of the impacts will stagger your aim.
The Thumper has two ranged weapons: a large telescopic cannon on its front, and a rapid-fire autocannon on one side.

## Tusk Thumper Bull
An upsized version of the Grineer mobile defense platform. Pneumatic groundpounders, high-drop entrances and wide-bore cannons deliver the same simple message: boom.

### Wiki
Grineer
An upsized version of the Grineer mobile defense platform. Pneumatic groundpounders, high-drop entrances and wide-bore cannons deliver the same simple message: boom.
The Tusk Thumper Bull is a more advanced variant of the Tusk Thumper encountered in the Plains of Eidolon during daytime.
A stronger variant of this enemy exists, the Tusk Thumper Doma.
Tactics
The heavy armor on the Tusk Thumper makes its body impervious to all forms of attack. To deal damage to it, players must destroy the destructible armor plates on the Thumper's knees, which will reveal a glowing bluish-green weakspot on each leg that can be attacked. Each weakspot constitutes a quarter of the Thumper's health, with each leg weakspot closing up if its health pool is depleted, thus all 4 leg weakspots must be destroyed in order to kill the Thumper.
The Thumper possesses unusual agility for a unit of its size, capable of jumping high into the air to either reposition itself, or attempt to slam down onto enemies beneath it. It is also capable of charging forward at great speeds, knocking over any enemies in its path.
Occasionally, the Thumper will brace its legs and then use its pneumatic groundpounder on its belly, which it slams into the ground repeatedly to create up to 5 radial shockwaves that deals damage and knockback in a large area. The Thumper in this state is immobile however, making it easier for players to aim for its weakspots.
The Thumper has two ranged weapons consisting of a large telescopic cannon on its front, and two rapid-fire autocannons on its sides. These weapons have their own hitboxes and hitpoints, which can be destroyed to temporarily knock them out of commission.
Dormant Thumpers are buried underground, and thus are not visible from long distances.

## Tyl Regor
Melee Specialist

### Wiki
Tyl Regor
Tyl Regor is the Grineer boss of the Uranus system. As the head researcher of Grineer gene repair, Regor works to remove the Cloning Decay Syndrome from his brethren, making strides through his "tubemen." Regor is fought in the mission Titania, Uranus. After defeating him and finishing the mission, the player will receive Equinox component blueprints.
Lore
General
As part of his efforts to cure the Grineer's Cloning Decay Syndrome, Tyl Regor has built secret underwater research facilities in the murky depths of Uranus's ocean. His research soon bears fruit in the form of "tubemen", specimens of Grineer that are stronger and healthier than normal Grineer.
Rathuum
Once, Tyl Regor experimented on his volunteer test subject, Dhurnam, in his endeavor to develop an experimental body augmentation. Unfortunately, the results were only half successful as the augmentation instead left him sluggish and unresponsive, only becoming fit for combat when he receives pain. Unfit for duty, the former test subject became an Executioner in the pits of Rathuum, the only place where he could find the steady diet of torment and suffering he needed to survive.
Operation: Tubemen of Regor
Tyl Regor's research however attracts the attention of Alad V, who now seemingly seeks to undo the Infested he is afflicted with, and sees Regor's research as the key to his cure. In turn, Alad V's intentions to acquire Regor's research for himself alarms Nef Anyo, who sees both the tubemen and Alad V as a threat to his position in the Corpus Board. Alad V and Nef Anyo make deals with the Tenno in order to raid Regor's laboratories to either retrieve or destroy Regor's research.

## Vapos Aquila
Nimble and relentless, the Aquila is the staple dogfighter of the Corpus fleet.

### Wiki
Corpus
Nimble and relentless, the Aquila is the staple dogfighter of the Corpus fleet.
The Vapos Aquila is a Corpus fighter that is encountered in the Corpus Gas City.
Tactics
They will spawn in certain open areas of the Gas City Tileset, and will open fire on any player it sees as it passes by.
**Tips:**
Any weapon that does Radiation damage can easily deal with Vapos Aquilas.
**Notes:**
Helios can scan the Aquila, but it seems to be inconsistent as it flies fast and usually over 50 metres away, out of its range.
**Trivia:**
Aquila means "eagle" in Latin; in line with Corpus proxies generally having bird-themed names.
The Aquila was the first Corpus Empyrean enemy players could encounter prior to Update 29.10.
Compared to the Aquila's concept art, the Vapos Aquila lacks the attachments for different weapons, suggesting its blasters are the fighter's "standard" arsenal.
Corpus Empyrean enemies such as the Basilisk, Harpi, and Weaver do feature the attachments shown in the concept art, confirming that they are upgraded Aquilas ships and that this is the standard model. However, the base Aquilas itself is absent from Empyrean missions.

## Ved Xol

### Wiki
Corpus
Ved Xol was nominated to become the head of the Loan Reclamation Division at Anyo Corp. after demonstrating ruthless aptitude within the Index. He has obtained favorable results with his team on many occasions, and is a firm favourite amongst corporate backers.
Behavior
Ved Xol uses his Opticor that can stagger any players on hit and deals significant damage.
If the player gets close to him (around 10 meters), he will perform a powerful ground slam that inflicts Electricity damage, as well as the proc, similar to Powerfists.
Also if the player is close enough, he will wield his Prova to attack.
Ved Xol can dodge if the player is aiming at or targeting him.
Ved Xol can summon up to 3 Nemes to aid him in combat.
His Arson Aura sets allies' Heat damage type modifier to +100%, causing them to take increased damage from Heat damage. However, it decreases allies' damage type modifier to Blast by -50%, causing them to take less Blast damage.

## Violence
Fast and vicious

### Wiki
Fast and vicious
Violence is one of Shadow Stalker's acolytes, first introduced during Operation: Shadow Debt. He takes the form of Limbo wearing Stalker's helmet and the pieces from the Hulta armor set.
As of Update 29.5, Violence can now spawn in The Steel Path missions.
Tactics
Violence wields a Destreza in his right hand, and a single Venka claw on his left hand, which deals Slash damage with an extremely high chance to proc. He can also cast Frost's Ice Wave and Excalibur's Slash Dash. Like the regular Slash Dash, Violence is invincible during the attack, regardless of what other effects are affecting him.
Violence also casts Silence periodically. For its duration, Silence affects Tenno within 20 meters of Violence, disabling the effects of many abilities and preventing further abilities from being cast.
Quotes
"(Player), your arrogance will be your demise."
"(Player), your Lotus will watch you perish."
"We will persist."
**Tips:**
If you cast your own Silence first, it will prevent Violence from casting abilities, including his own Silence.
**Trivia:**
When used against the Adaptation mod, Violence's Ice Wave will grant protection against Viral damage instead of Cold damage.
Health: 350 | Shield: 200 | Armor: 0 | Faction: Stalker

## Zanuka
Fast with claw and missile attacks

### Wiki
Corpus
For the Assassin, see Zanuka Hunter
For the variant found in The Index with its Helios Companion, see Lockjaw & Sol
Alad V
Zanuka is Alad V's robotic pet created with parts of various Warframes, serving as his proudly announced product of the Zanuka Project. Both Alad V and Zanuka are encountered together on Themisto, Jupiter.
Behavior
Zanuka appears as a cyan armored robotic canine. It is highly aggressive and agile, capable of doing dodge rolls and jumping around terrain. It will continuously chase the Tenno while launching attacks. Aside from the basic claw strikes, it has several ranged abilities.
Missile Strike: Launches several guided missiles, doing moderate damage. Due to their powerful guiding capabilities, the most reliable way to dodge them is still to hide behind cover.
Frost Bomb: Launches a gray spherical bomb that explodes, inflicting Cold damage and proc.
Dispel Bomb: Launches a spherical bomb that explodes, dispelling abilities. Uses when a Tenno has deployed a self-buff such as Iron Skin.
Revive Alad V: Whenever Alad V's health is depleted before Zanuka is killed, Alad V collapses and Zanuka will immediately rush to his aid to revive him, restoring their shields if successful.
Strategy
Alad V cannot be taken down until Zanuka is slain first. However, its shields have innate damage reduction and therefore are extremely durable, but will vanish once Alad V's own shields are depleted. Avoid using Toxin damage against Alad, as they ignore shields.
In the event Alad V's health is depleted before Zanuka is killed, he will collapse in a state similar to bleedout, and Zanuka will immediately rush to his aid and bring him back on his feet, restoring a portion of his shields. Although Zanuka becomes an easier target when attempting to rescue Alad, it may be easier to ignore Alad after his shields are down and attack Zanuka until it is destroyed.
Health: 1000 | Shield: 15000 | Armor: 25 | Faction: Corpus

## Zanuka Hunter
Captures Warframes

### Wiki
Corpus
Alad V
The Zanuka Hunter, formerly known as the Harvester, is a gray-colored Zanuka proxy controlled by Alad V. During missions, it will attack one targeted Tenno until either the Tenno or Zanuka Hunter dies. It appears rarely, in a manner similar to the Stalker. Triggering a Zanuka Hunter attack requires a player to have a Death Mark, acquired by fighting against the Corpus across five Invasion missions, or it can be summoned by using a Zanuka Hunter Beacon. Active Death Marks can be seen on a player's profile.
Players who have acquired a death-mark will receive an inbox message from Alad V. The text of the message reads:
"Thank you for volunteering for the Zanuka Project, you will make a fine addition to my collection."
Any subsequently acquired death-marks will have a different inbox message from Alad V that instead reads:
"Thank you for your continued interest in the Zanuka Project. A member of our staff will assist you shortly..."
The Zanuka Hunter only spawns in Corpus missions (supporting the Grineer during invasions against Corpus is considered a Corpus mission, and any mission where Corpus invades as a faction war). The Zanuka Hunter spawn chance rises +0.5 additively with each player in the party with a death-mark.
The Zanuka Hunter's spawning is announced by flickering lights, much like the Stalker's appearance. Also, your screen will become desaturated and your Warframe will flicker with light.
Taunts
The Zanuka Hunter has most of its speech represented by Alad V. Here is the list of his phrases:
Deploying
"..."
During Fight
"Tenno? Can you hear me? You've been a naughty little betrayer, haven't you? Did you really think your support for the Grineer would go unnoticed?
"This is really for your own good, Tenno. Help me, help you... help me! (Laughs)"
"That is just the sort of behavior I just can't let go uncorrected. Otherwise, you'll never learn.
Player Captured
"Good job, Zanuka. This Tenno's bones will make you a fine sibling."

## Zeplen
Locks opponents in a tractor field preventing escape

### Wiki
Grineer
Locks opponents in a tractor field preventing escape
The Zeplen is a Grineer space support unit which entraps nearby Tenno within a large energy net that prevents them from escaping; destroying the Zeplen removes the energy net. Zeplens are also capable of launching missiles against players.
The Zeplen is encountered in the final mission of the Archwing Quest, in Operation Eyes of Blight, and any Balor Fomorian invasion.
**Notes:**
Tenno can enter the Zeplen's energy net from the outside, but will be trapped within the net once entered.
Zeplen can also trap any outgoing firepower from inside its energy net. However, attacks from outside the net can enter and hit objects within.
The Zeplen has the highest amount of base armor of any unit in the game.
Has the same model as the Missile Battery.
**Trivia:**
The Zeplen is named after the Zeppelin, a type of airship. These airships primarily saw use as scouts and bombing craft during the First World War.
